File directory: services-integration-shlib-sfsc-standard-updates/apps/services-integration-shlib-sfsc-standard-updates/SHLIB_StandardUpdates/PaymentRequest_updateSAPPayment_updateStatus.esql

BROKER SCHEMA SHLIB_StandardUpdates


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE PaymentRequest_updateSAPPayment_updateStatus
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE Ref REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables.PaymentRequestId;
		/* 
		response SFSC after update
		[
		    {
		        "id": "0t7FT00000000MbYAI",
		        "success": true,
		        "errors": []
		    },
		    {
		        "id": "0t7FT00000000MbYAI",
		        "success": true,
		        "errors": []
		    }
		]
		*/
		
		/* 
		Before loop
		{
	        "0t7FT00000000MbYAI": {
	            "statusSfcasBeforeUpdate": "Paid"
	        },
	        "0t7FT00000000ODYAY": {
	            "statusSfcasBeforeUpdate": "Payé"
	        },
	        "0t7bW00000002TFQAY": {
	            "statusSfcasBeforeUpdate": "INIT"
	        }
	    }
	    After Loop
	    {
	        "0t7FT00000000MbYAI": {
	            "statusSfcasBeforeUpdate": "Paid",
	            "success": true,
	            "errors": {}
	        },
	        "0t7FT00000000ODYAY": {
	            "statusSfcasBeforeUpdate": "Payé",
	            "success": true,
	            "errors": {}
	        },
	        "0t7bW00000002TFQAY": {
	            "statusSfcasBeforeUpdate": "INIT"
	        }
    
		}
		*/
		for UpdatePayment as InputRoot.JSON.Data.Item[] do
			DECLARE id CHAR UpdatePayment.id;
			if id is not null then
				set Ref.{UpdatePayment.id}.success value = UpdatePayment.success;
				set Ref.{UpdatePayment.id}.errors = UpdatePayment.errors;
			end if;
		end for;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: services-integration-shlib-sfsc-standard-updates/apps/services-integration-shlib-sfsc-standard-updates/SHLIB_StandardUpdates/UpdateDocumentLinks_prepareApisCalls.esql

BROKER SCHEMA SHLIB_StandardUpdates


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE UpdateDocumentLinks_prepareApisCalls
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		set OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		call PrepareContextOauth(Environment);
		SET Environment.Variables.Request.API.Endpoint.Method = 'POST'; 
		SET Environment.Variables.Request.API.Endpoint.Querypath =  Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy('{services-integration-policies-properties-sfsc-standard-updates}:Common' , 'Endpoint_CRUD');
		
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;


================================================================================

File directory: services-integration-shlib-sfsc-standard-updates/apps/services-integration-shlib-sfsc-standard-updates/SHLIB_StandardUpdates/UpdateDocumentLinks_transformation.esql

BROKER SCHEMA SHLIB_StandardUpdates


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE UpdateDocumentLinks_transformation
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE InitialObject CHAR Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy('{services-integration-policies-properties-sfsc-standard-updates}:Common' , 'UpdateDocumentLinks');
		-- prepare context to call CallApiWithToken
		call PrepareContextOauth(Environment);
		SET Environment.Variables.Request.API.Endpoint.Method = 'POST'; 
		SET Environment.Variables.Request.API.Endpoint.Querypath =  Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy('{services-integration-policies-properties-sfsc-standard-updates}:Common' , 'Endpoint_CRUD');
				
		-- @@description@@
		set InitialObject = REPLACE(InitialObject,'@@description@@',InputRoot.JSON.Data.override.DESCRIPTION);
		-- @@title@@
		set InitialObject = REPLACE(InitialObject,'@@title@@',InputRoot.JSON.Data.override.TITLE);
		-- @@id_external_datasource@@
		set InitialObject = REPLACE(InitialObject,'@@id_external_datasource@@',InputRoot.JSON.Data.override.ID_EXTERNAL_DATASOURCE);
		-- @@content_url@@
		set InitialObject = REPLACE(InitialObject,'@@content_url@@',InputRoot.JSON.Data.override.PARAMETERS_URL);
		-- @@parameters_url@@@@parameters_url@@
		set InitialObject = REPLACE(InitialObject,'@@parameters_url@@',InputRoot.JSON.Data.override.PARAMETERS_URL);
		-- @@onbase_id@@
		set InitialObject = REPLACE(InitialObject,'@@onbase_id@@',InputRoot.JSON.Data.override.ONBASE_ID);
		-- @@tag_csv@@
		set InitialObject = REPLACE(InitialObject,'@@tag_csv@@',InputRoot.JSON.Data.override.TAG_CSV);
		-- @@object_id@@
		set InitialObject = REPLACE(InitialObject,'@@object_id@@',InputRoot.JSON.Data.override.OBJECT_ID);
		
		CREATE FIELD OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
		call AttachObj(InitialObject,OutputRoot.JSON.Data);
		
		-- prepare context to call CallApiWithToken
		call PrepareContextOauth(Environment);
		SET Environment.Variables.Request.API.Endpoint.Method = 'POST'; 
		SET Environment.Variables.Request.API.Endpoint.Querypath =  Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy('{services-integration-policies-properties-sfsc-standard-updates}:Common' , 'Endpoint_CRUD');
				
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

END MODULE;

File directory: services-integration-shlib-sfsc-standard-updates/apps/services-integration-shlib-sfsc-standard-updates/SHLIB_StandardUpdates/utils.esql

BROKER SCHEMA SHLIB_StandardUpdates


CREATE PROCEDURE AttachObj(IN ObjectChar CHARACTER,IN PathToAttach REFERENCE) BEGIN
	DECLARE dataAsBit BIT  CAST(ObjectChar AS BIT CCSID 1208);
	DECLARE RowTmp ROW;
	CREATE LASTCHILD OF RowTmp DOMAIN 'JSON' PARSE(dataAsBit CCSID 1208);
	
	set PathToAttach = RowTmp.JSON.Data;
	
END;

CREATE PROCEDURE PrepareContextOauth(IN Environment REFERENCE) BEGIN
		SET Environment.Variables.Request.Cache.Name = 'sfsc';
		
		-- TOKEN information -------------------
		CREATE FIELD Environment.Variables.Request.Token;
		DECLARE tokenInfo REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables.Request.Token;

		CREATE LASTCHILD OF tokenInfo NAME 'Headers';
		DECLARE headers REFERENCE TO tokenInfo.Headers;
		SET headers.TYPE = JSON.Array;
		SET headers.Item[1].key = 'Content-Type';
		SET headers.Item[1].value = 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=utf-8';
		SET headers.Item[2].key = 'Accept';
		SET headers.Item[2].value = '*/*';

		SET tokenInfo."Policy"."Project" = 'services-integration-policies-credentials'; 
		SET tokenInfo."Policy"."Name" = 'SfcasCredentialsPolicy'; 

		SET tokenInfo."Endpoint"."Name" = 'path-support-it-api-security-sfdc-oauth'; 
		SET tokenInfo."Endpoint"."policy_project" = '{services-integration-policies-endpoints}:ApicEndPoints'; 
		DECLARE policy_name CHAR '{' || tokenInfo.Policy.Project || '}:' || tokenInfo.Policy.Name;
		SET tokenInfo.Message = 'client_id=' || Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy(policy_name, 'client_id') ||
		                        '&client_secret=' || Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy(policy_name, 'client_secret') ||
		                        '&grant_type=' || Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy(policy_name, 'grant_type') ||
		                        '&username=' || Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy(policy_name, 'oauth_username') ||
		                        '&password=' || Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy(policy_name, 'oauth_password');
		                                                  
				
		-- API information -------------------
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.Request.API;
		SET Environment.Variables.Request.API."Endpoint"."Name" = 'path-external-api-sfsc-standard-apis';
		SET Environment.Variables.Request.API."Endpoint"."policy_project" = '{services-integration-policies-endpoints}:ApicEndPoints'; 

		CREATE LASTCHILD OF Environment.Variables.Request.API AS headers NAME 'Headers';
		DECLARE headersApi REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables.Request.API.Headers;
		SET headers.TYPE = JSON.Array;
		SET headers.Item[1].key = 'Accept';
		SET headers.Item[1].value = 'application/json';
		SET headers.Item[2].key = 'Content-Type';
		SET headers.Item[2].value = 'application/json; charset=utf-8';
		
	
END;

File directory: common-integration-shlib-oauth-api-call/apps/common-integration-shlib-oauth-api-call/readme.txt

Pré-requis :  API technique pour la récupération du token + 1 API fonctionnelle.
Les paths de ces APIs doivent être définis dans la policy 'APICEndPointPolicies'.

L'accès à l'API Token se fait à l'aide d'un fichier Policy qui contient les paramètres de sécurité d'accès (possibilité de surcharger avec les infos Vault). 
  Voir par exemple 'external-api-sparx-authentication' dans le repository APIC.
  Et le fichier policy associé 'common-acepolicies-sparxcredentialspolicies' dans le repository ACE.
  
L'accès à l'API fonctionnelle se fait avec les accréditations de l'application ACE. Le token est passé automatiquement à l'appel.
  Voir par exemple 'external-api-sparx-assistance-cases' dans le repository APIC.

Exemple d'utilisation de la shared library :
  Voir par exemple 'services-integration-assistance-cases-events-sparx-consume' dans le repository ACE.
  
  1. Utiliser un ComputeNode pour positionner la structure utilisée par la shared library.
		-- Nom du cache. Doit être unique par système externe
  		SET Environment.Variables.Request.Cache.Name = 'sparx';
		
		-- message envoyé lors de la demande du token
		SET Environment.Variables.Request.Token.Message = 'grant_type=client_credentials&scope=arcnet.incidents.read%20arcnet.incidents.write%20arcnet.organisations.read%20arcnet.organisations.write';

		DECLARE tokenInfo REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables.Request.Token;

		-- possibilité d'ajouter des headers lors de l'appel du token. A noter que le clientId/secret seront positionnés avec le contenu du fichier policy 
		CREATE LASTCHILD OF tokenInfo AS headers NAME 'Headers';
		SET headers.TYPE = JSON.Array;
		SET headers.Item[1].key='Content-Type';
		SET headers.Item[1].value='application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=utf-8';
		SET headers.Item[2].key='Accept';
		SET headers.Item[2].value='*/*';

		-- nom du projet Policy à utiliser pour l'accès à l'API technique qui récupère le token
		SET tokenInfo."Policy"."Project" = 'SparxCredentialsPolicies'; 
		SET tokenInfo."Policy"."Name" = 'SparxCredentialsPolicy'; 

		-- nom de l'entrée dasn APICEndPointPolicies ou est décrit l'API technique qui récupère le token
		SET tokenInfo."Endpoint"."Name" = 'basepath-external-api-sparx-authentication'; 
		-- et nom du fichier policy ou extraire cette information
		SET tokenInfo."Endpoint"."policy_project" = '{APICEndpointPolicies}:APICEndpointPolicy'; 

		-- nom de l'entrée dasn APICEndPointPolicies ou est décrit l'API fonctionnelle
		SET Environment.Variables.Request.API."Endpoint"."Name" = 'basepath-external-api-sparx-cases'; 
		-- et nom du fichier policy associé
		SET Environment.Variables.Request.API.Endpoint."policy_project" = '{APICEndpointPolicies}:APICEndpointPolicy'; 

  2. Inclure l'appel au subflow 'CallApiWithToken'


Structure utilisée en interne:
- Environment.Variables.Request.Cache.Name(*) : clé pour le cache du token complet. Unique par provider.

- Environment.Variables.Request.Token : contient les variables nécessaires à l'extraction du token.
  * Headers : une array json contenant les headers à passer
    - key
	- value
  * Policy : les informations de la policy à utiliser pour la récupération du token. Le fichier policy doit contenir les entrées 'oauth_username', 'oauth_password', 'oauth_clientid', 'oauth_clientsecret'
    - Project(*) : nom du projet
	- Name(*) : nom de la policy
  * Endpoint : les informations du endpoint pour le token
    - Name(*) : nom de l'entrée dans la policy global APICEndpointPolicy
	- Querypath : path à ajouter à la fin de l'url
	- Method : HTTP méthode à utiliser (POST par défaut)
  * Message : payload pour la requête du token
	
- Environment.Variables.Request.API : contient les variables nécessaires à l'appel de l'API.
  * Headers : une array json contenant les headers à passer
    - key
	- value
  * Endpoint : les informations du endpoint pour le token
    - Name(*) : nom de l'entrée dans la policy global APICEndpointPolicy
	- Querypath : path à ajouter à la fin de l'url
	- Method : HTTP méthode à utiliser (POST par défaut)
  
- Environment.Variables.Request.Save.Message : temporaire. Contient la sauvegarde du payload pour l'API

================================================================================

File directory: common-integration-shlib-oauth-api-call/apps/common-integration-shlib-oauth-api-call/SHLIB_oAuthApiCall/CallApiWithToken_Error401.esql

BROKER SCHEMA SHLIB_oAuthApiCall



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE CallApiWithToken_Error401
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		CALL CopyMessageHeaders();
		CALL CopyEntireMessage();
		
		IF InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader."X-Original-HTTP-Status-Code" = 401 THEN
			PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out1';
        	RETURN FALSE; -- stop the 'out' flow
		END IF;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyMessageHeaders() BEGIN
		DECLARE I INTEGER 1;
		DECLARE J INTEGER;
		SET J = CARDINALITY(InputRoot.*[]);
		WHILE I < J DO
			SET OutputRoot.*[I] = InputRoot.*[I];
			SET I = I + 1;
		END WHILE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyEntireMessage() BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: common-integration-shlib-oauth-api-call/apps/common-integration-shlib-oauth-api-call/SHLIB_oAuthApiCall/CallFinalAPI_Prepare_API_Request.esql

BROKER SCHEMA SHLIB_oAuthApiCall


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE CallFinalAPI_Prepare_API_Request
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE cacheName CHARACTER;
		DECLARE environ REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables.Request;
		
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment; -- keep the environment
		
		-- store in cache
		SET cacheName = Environment.Variables.Request.Cache.Name;
		
		-- restore environment
		CREATE LASTCHILD of OutputRoot NAME 'HTTPRequestHeader';
		CALL AddHeadersForRequest (environ.API, OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader, InputRoot, Environment);
		-- add Bearer token
		SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader."Authorization" = CACHE_TOKEN.{cacheName}.{'token'};
		-- add ACE credentials
		SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader."x-ibm-client-id" = Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy('{AceAppEsbCredentialsPolicies}:AceAppEsbCredentialsPolicy', 'client_id');
		SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader."x-ibm-client-secret" = Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy('{AceAppEsbCredentialsPolicies}:AceAppEsbCredentialsPolicy', 'client_secret');
		
		-- change url
		DECLARE urlCalled CHARACTER;					
        SET urlCalled = Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy('{GlobalEndpointsPolicies}:GlobalEndpointsPolicy', 'apic-host') || Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy(environ.API.Endpoint."policy_project", environ.API.Endpoint.Name);
        IF EXISTS(environ.API.Endpoint.Querypath[]) THEN
        	SET urlCalled = urlCalled || environ.API.Endpoint.Querypath;
        END IF;
        SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestURL = urlCalled;
        IF EXISTS(environ.API.Endpoint.Method[]) THEN
	        SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestLine.Method = environ.API.Endpoint.Method;
        END IF; 
		
		CASE FIELDNAME(environ.Save.Message.*[<])
		WHEN 'JSON' THEN
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data = environ.Save.Message.JSON.Data;
		WHEN 'BLOB' THEN
			SET OutputRoot.BLOB.BLOB = environ.Save.Message.BLOB.BLOB;
		ELSE
			CREATE LASTCHILD of OutputRoot FROM environ.Save.Message.*[<];
		END CASE;

		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

END MODULE;

File directory: common-integration-shlib-oauth-api-call/apps/common-integration-shlib-oauth-api-call/SHLIB_oAuthApiCall/RequestHelper.esql

BROKER SCHEMA SHLIB_oAuthApiCall




CREATE PROCEDURE AddHeadersForRequest(IN inref REFERENCE, IN outref REFERENCE, IN main_input REFERENCE, IN main_env REFERENCE)
BEGIN
	-- add all headers defined under inref into outref
	IF EXISTS(inref.Headers[]) then
		FOR oneHeader AS inref.Headers.Item[] DO
			CREATE LASTCHILD of outref NAME oneHeader.key VALUE oneHeader.value;
		END FOR;
	END IF;
	IF NOT EXISTS(outref."GTID"[]) THEN
		SET outref."GTID" = COALESCE(main_input.MQRFH2.usr.GTID, COALESCE(COALESCE(main_input.HTTPInputHeader."X-Global-Transaction-Id", main_input.MQRFH2.usr.(MQRFH2.Field)GTID), COALESCE(main_env.ESB.ESBEnvelope.GTID, COALESCE(main_env.Variables.TCSLog.context."global-transaction-id", UUIDASCHAR))));
		SET outref."X-Global-Transaction-Id" = outref."GTID";
		SET outref."TID" = COALESCE(COALESCE(main_input.HTTPInputHeader."X-Request-ID", main_input.MQRFH2.usr.(MQRFH2.Field)TID), COALESCE(main_env.ESB.ESBEnvelope.TID, UUIDASCHAR));
		SET outref."CID" = COALESCE(COALESCE(main_input.HTTPInputHeader."X-Correlation-ID", main_input.MQRFH2.usr.(MQRFH2.Field)CID), COALESCE(main_env.ESB.ESBEnvelope.CID,''));
	END IF;		
END;

================================================================================

File directory: common-integration-shlib-oauth-api-call/apps/common-integration-shlib-oauth-api-call/SHLIB_oAuthApiCall/RetrieveToken_Filter.esql

BROKER SCHEMA SHLIB_oAuthApiCall


CREATE FILTER MODULE RetrieveToken_Filter
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE reloadCache BOOLEAN true;
        DECLARE cacheName CHARACTER;
        DECLARE cacheToken CHARACTER;
        DECLARE cacheExpiration CHARACTER;
        
        SET cacheName = Environment.Variables.Request.Cache.Name;
        IF EXISTS(CACHE_TOKEN.{cacheName}[]) THEN
        	SET cacheToken = CACHE_TOKEN.{cacheName}.{'token'};
        	SET cacheExpiration = CACHE_TOKEN.{cacheName}.{'expiration'};
        	IF cacheToken IS NOT NULL THEN
        		IF cacheExpiration IS NOT NULL THEN
        			DECLARE dtExpiration TIMESTAMP;
        			SET dtExpiration = CAST(cacheExpiration AS TIMESTAMP);
        			IF dtExpiration > CURRENT_TIMESTAMP THEN
	    				SET reloadCache = false;
	    			END IF;
        		ELSE
    				-- a token without expiration 
    				SET reloadCache = false;
    			END IF;
        	END IF; 
        END IF;
		
		RETURN reloadCache;
	END;

END MODULE;

File directory: common-integration-shlib-oauth-api-call/apps/common-integration-shlib-oauth-api-call/SHLIB_oAuthApiCall/RetrieveToken_PrepareRequest.esql

BROKER SCHEMA SHLIB_oAuthApiCall


-- cache of Tokens. For each row, contains 2 values : 
--    'token' contains the token value prefixed by the token type : Bearer XCt6... 
--    'expiration' which is a date of expiration of the token. Represented as timestamp 
DECLARE CACHE_TOKEN SHARED ROW;

		
CREATE COMPUTE MODULE RetrieveToken_PrepareRequest
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		-- prepare root for token requisite
        CALL PrepareForTokenRequest();
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
	
	CREATE PROCEDURE PrepareForTokenRequest()
	BEGIN
		DECLARE environ REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables.Request;
				
		-- set HttpRequest parameters after Property and before Message		
		CREATE LASTCHILD of OutputRoot NAME 'HTTPRequestHeader';
		DECLARE headers REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader;
		
		CALL AddHeadersForRequest (environ.Token, headers, InputRoot, Environment);

		IF EXISTS(environ.Token.Message[]) then
			DECLARE charset INTEGER COALESCE(InputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId, 1208);
			IF charset = 0 THEN
				SET charset = 1208;
			END IF;
			SET OutputRoot.BLOB.BLOB = CAST(environ.Token.Message AS BLOB CCSID charset);
		END IF;
				

		DECLARE policy_name CHARACTER;
		DECLARE oauth_name CHARACTER;
		DECLARE oauth_password CHARACTER;
		
		SET policy_name = '{' || environ.Token.Policy.Project || '}:' || environ.Token.Policy.Name;
		SET oauth_name = Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy(policy_name, 'oauth_username');
		SET oauth_password = Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy(policy_name, 'oauth_password');
		 
		SET headers.Authorization = 'Basic ' || BASE64ENCODE(oauth_name || ':' || oauth_password);

		SET headers."x-ibm-client-id" = Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy('{AceAppEsbCredentialsPolicies}:AceAppEsbCredentialsPolicy', 'client_id');
		SET headers."x-ibm-client-secret" = Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy('{AceAppEsbCredentialsPolicies}:AceAppEsbCredentialsPolicy', 'client_secret');

		-- change url
		DECLARE urlCalled CHARACTER;
				
        SET urlCalled = Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy('{GlobalEndpointsPolicies}:GlobalEndpointsPolicy', 'apic-host') || Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy(environ.Token.Endpoint."policy_project", environ.Token.Endpoint.Name);
        IF EXISTS(environ.Token.Endpoint.Querypath[]) THEN
        	SET urlCalled = urlCalled || environ.Token.Endpoint.Querypath;
        END IF;
        SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestURL = urlCalled;
        IF EXISTS(environ.Token.Endpoint.Method[]) THEN
	        SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestLine.Method = environ.Token.Endpoint.Method;
        END IF; 
               
	END;

END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE ResetToken
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
        DECLARE cacheName CHARACTER;
        
        SET cacheName = Environment.Variables.Request.Cache.Name;

    	SET CACHE_TOKEN.{cacheName}.{'token'} = NULL;
    	SET CACHE_TOKEN.{cacheName}.{'expiration'} = NULL;
			
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: common-integration-shlib-oauth-api-call/apps/common-integration-shlib-oauth-api-call/SHLIB_oAuthApiCall/RetrieveToken_saveMessage.esql

BROKER SCHEMA SHLIB_oAuthApiCall


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE RetrieveToken_saveMessage
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		CALL CopyEntireMessage();
        
		-- keep actual message
		SET Environment.Variables.Request.Save.Message = InputRoot;
		
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyEntireMessage() BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: common-integration-shlib-oauth-api-call/apps/common-integration-shlib-oauth-api-call/SHLIB_oAuthApiCall/RetrieveToken_StoreToken.esql

BROKER SCHEMA SHLIB_oAuthApiCall



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE RetrieveToken_StoreToken
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE cacheName CHARACTER;
        DECLARE resultTokenKey CHARACTER;
        DECLARE resultExpirationKey CHARACTER;
		DECLARE environ REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables.Request;
		
		-- store in cache
		SET cacheName = Environment.Variables.Request.Cache.Name;

        SET CACHE_TOKEN.{cacheName}.{'token'} = InputRoot.JSON.Data."token_type" || ' ' || InputRoot.JSON.Data."access_token";

		IF COALESCE(Environment.Variables.Request.Token.override_token_expires_in, '') <> ''
		THEN
			-- refresh/renew token before it actually expires
			SET CACHE_TOKEN.{cacheName}.{'expiration'} = CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP + CAST(Environment.Variables.Request.Token.override_token_expires_in AS INTERVAL SECOND) AS CHARACTER FORMAT 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
		ELSEIF EXISTS(InputRoot.JSON.Data."expires_in"[]) THEN
	        SET CACHE_TOKEN.{cacheName}.{'expiration'} = CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP + CAST(InputRoot.JSON.Data.expires_in AS INTERVAL SECOND) AS CHARACTER FORMAT 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
		ELSE
			SET CACHE_TOKEN.{cacheName}.{'expiration'} = NULL;
		END IF;
			
		--------------------------------------------------------------------
		
		
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

END MODULE;

File directory: common-integration-shlib-policies-reader/apps/common-integration-shlib-policies-reader/Shlib_PoliciesReader/GetUDPEsqlFunction.esql

BROKER SCHEMA Shlib_PoliciesReader

CREATE FUNCTION gestionPolicy( IN policyName CHARACTER, IN propertyName CHARACTER ) RETURNS CHARACTER
BEGIN
	DECLARE prop CHAR getPolicyPropertyWithOutError(policyName, propertyName);
	if prop = 'POLICY_NOT_FOUND' OR prop = 'PROPERTY_NOT_FOUND' THEN 
		THROW USER EXCEPTION CATALOG 'MyCatalog' MESSAGE 4004 VALUES(policyName || '/' || propertyName || ' not found');
	end if;
	return prop;

END;
CREATE PROCEDURE getAllPolicyProperties (IN PolicyProjectName CHAR,IN PolicyName CHAR, IN rowRef REFERENCE )  
BEGIN
	/* 
		exemple d'appel  
		call getAllPolicyProperties('DigitalIntakePolicies','BALOISEDIG',OutputRoot.JSON.Data.test);
	*/
	DECLARE properties CHAR getAllPolicyPropertiesJava('{' || PolicyProjectName || '}:' ||  PolicyName);
	DECLARE RowTempo ROW;
	DECLARE dataAsBit BIT  CAST(properties AS BIT CCSID 1208);
	CREATE LASTCHILD OF RowTempo DOMAIN 'JSON' PARSE(dataAsBit CCSID 1208);
	set rowRef = RowTempo.JSON.Data;
	
END;
CREATE FUNCTION getPolicyProperty( IN policyName CHARACTER, IN propertyName CHARACTER )
RETURNS CHARACTER
LANGUAGE JAVA
EXTERNAL NAME "GETUDP_JavaCompute.getPolicyProperty";

CREATE FUNCTION getPolicyPropertyWithOutError( IN policyName CHARACTER, IN propertyName CHARACTER )
RETURNS CHARACTER
LANGUAGE JAVA
EXTERNAL NAME "GETUDP_JavaCompute.getPolicyPropertyWithOutError";

CREATE FUNCTION getAllPolicyPropertiesJava( IN policyName CHARACTER )
RETURNS CHARACTER
LANGUAGE JAVA
EXTERNAL NAME "GETUDP_JavaCompute.getAllPolicyProperties";
